**Count: 34 knob rows** (the floor is 30). Rows for `DIAG_DUMP_DIR`, `OVERLAY_STRIP_ANCHOR`, `OVERLAY_LAUNCH_OPTION`, the key rows, `DIAG_REVEAL_CHORD`, `DIAG_FILTER_COUNTER_SEVERITY` and `DIAG_STRIP_FIELDS` are configuration rather than tuning. Differences from the specialist proposal, each an orchestrator ruling: `DIAG_SILENT_LIE_GRACE_FRAMES` default 240 to 120 and range to 30 to 600, because the reach-age gate (DO-21 row 4) now absorbs the slow-onset case the old default was sized for and the grace covers only rounding, wander and a one-run invalidation latency; `DIAG_SKEW_GRACE_FRAMES` default 12 to 24, so the allowance spans two Sanity runs at 60 fps (a run is 12 frames) instead of exactly one; `DIAG_POST_RESTORE_SETTLE_FRAMES` default 2 to 6, so the settle window spans at least one 10 Hz refresh and `SETTLING` can actually be observed; `DIAG_FILTER_CPU_WINDOW_FRAMES` added by CP-9.

**Derived or cited values the Overlay does not own (point at the source of truth; never copy):** `GLIDE_REACH_FRAC`, `WANDER_BLEND_S`, `SWAP_LEAD_S`, `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE`, `D_VIS_MIN_DEG`, `D_MAX_FRAC_ARC`, `RM_GLIDE_SCALE` and `t_return_max(lie)` belong to the Perception Filter (pf D.2, D.6, Tuning Knobs); `LEAK_EPS_DEG` 0.25 and `LEAK_FADE_S` 1.5 are the registry's `lie_leak_amplitude`; the per-field quanta (`T` 1000 mC, `P` 10 dkPa, `p_o2` 5 dkPa, store 20 permille, intensity 100 permille, `hp` 250 bp) belong to the Graph (LS-20, lsg:201) and the Suit vitals buckets to the Suit (suit-and-equipment.md:270-277 (SE-38)); `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256, `QUEUE_CAPACITY` 1024, `PER_ISSUER_CAPACITY` 256, `MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME` 4, `COMMAND_LOG_CAPACITY` 1024, `TICK_RATE` 60, `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4 (a threshold, not a cap) belong to the kernel (registry entries); `GRAPH_HOOK_BUDGET_MS` (0.5 P99 / 1.0 max per run, MVP, on the Release export template) belongs to the Graph; `CPU_BUDGET_MS` 0.5 (a median per frame) belongs to the Filter; `SANITY_DRIFT_DIVISOR` 12 and `ONSET_DELAY_RUNS` 10 to 25 belong to Sanity; `H_default = max(k, 3)` belongs to the Pipeline; `LIGHT_CPU_BUDGET_US` 1000 belongs to Lighting.

**Registry candidates that are not knobs** (written by the merge stage from this final text, never from a draft): `overlay_source_root` (path, placeholder `src/debug/`), `overlay_build_gate`, `dev_artifact_list`, `diag_panel_contract`, and the nine formulas `desync_classifier`, `graph_hook_sum_run`, `hook_window_p99`, `dirty_load_pm`, `queue_load_pm`, `ticks_per_overlay_refresh`, `overlay_cpu_us`, `hash_cost_per_s`, `stage_entry_latency_s`. The `DIAG_*` / `OVERLAY_*` knobs above are proposed as dev-presentation entries; ★ `DIAG_HASH_CADENCE_TICKS` is the only one that is a cross-system fact.

## Visual/Audio Requirements

The Overlay has **no world rendering of its own and no audio**. What is visible is a flat, unlit, text-first dev layer drawn by the Pixel-Perfect Render Pipeline & Camera at pipeline layer 4 in the root viewport (integer-scaled at `H_default = max(k, 3)`, no Filter pass, no lighting, no camera jolt, no grade; DO-5), plus an optional set of world debug draws that the owners' channels toggle on the Pipeline's dev-only layer-0 debug node (DO-26). **Which GDD renders it:** the Pipeline owns the layer slot, the scale and the `DEBUG_OVERLAY` render-state modifier; this GDD owns the content (the pages, the tags, the palette use); each sibling owns the draw callback behind its own debug-draw toggle. **Art bible sections that apply:** section 3.4 (UI grammar: linear bars with a needle mark, never a ring, arc or dial), section 4.6 (the UI palette: Ground Graphite `#1C2128`, frame `#5C6670`, Hull Grey `#8C949C`, Paper White `#DDE4EA`, Tungsten `#FFCC66`, and Alarm Red `#E0102A` kept exclusively for alarm so that rarity is the signal, ab:375, ab:463), the reserved-hue scan (violet and flesh belong to sanity leaks and are never used here, ab:1207), R-7 (never an accessibility feature, ab:886) and ART 7.4 (the true-value overlay is never an accessibility option, restated at chart-table-web-map-and-menus.md:544).

| Event | Visual feedback | Audio feedback | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|
| Mode `OFF` to `FULL` (`toggle`) | The Graphite plate and the current page appear on the next rendered frame; no fade, no slide (DO-6) | None | MVP (dev) |
| Page change (`page_next`, `page_prev`) | The adjacent page replaces the previous on the next frame; the page name and viewer id stay in the header | None | MVP (dev) |
| A row becomes `OK` / `NOTE` / `WARN` | The text tag only (`OK`, `NOTE`, `WARN`) in Paper White; `WARN` uses no red and no fill | None | MVP (dev) |
| A row becomes `DEFECT` | The text tag `DEFECT` plus a small Alarm Red mark beside it; the mark is the only red on the page and is never a fill, bar or accent; a greyscale view still shows the tag (DO-27) | None (no beep, no sonification; the Player Fantasy guard rail "Never audible", DO-30) | MVP (dev) |
| `DELIBERATE` lie shown | `NOTE` tagged `LIE`: the true value in Paper White beside the perceived value in Hull Grey, never tinted by the lie, never violet or flesh (a lie is the game working, not a fault) | None | MVP (dev) |
| `NO_MASK` (`filter_pass_disabled`) | The `DEFECT` tag with the red mark (Pipeline pipe:735); `NOT_USED` is plain `OK`, never red | None | MVP (dev) |
| Kernel `FAULTED` in a dev build | The six-field FAULT record is pinned on `P-KERNEL` and on the strip; other pages freeze on their last good values; `HIDDEN` draws nothing (DO-11) | None | MVP (dev) |
| Kernel `LOADING` | A `LOADING` banner over the frozen last page | None | MVP (dev) |
| Observer strip shown (`mode`, or the `reveal` chord from `HIDDEN`) | One text line at `OVERLAY_STRIP_ANCHOR` on a Graphite plate: `T`, kernel state, Attention `phase`, sanity `stage`, hash status (DO-7) | None | MVP (dev) |
| Channel `REJECTED` / `SUSPENDED` / `STALE` | The status word and a `DEFECT` or `NOTE` row naming the `channel_id`; the last values stay visible, marked, when `STALE` | None | MVP (dev) |
| World debug draw toggled on (outlines, cones, rects, true positions) | Thin Hull Grey or Tungsten lines of at least `OVERLAY_DEBUG_DRAW_WIDTH_PX` 1 base pixel on the layer-0 dev node, drawn unshaded; toggled off, the world frame is bit-identical to a never-toggled frame | None | MVP (dev) |
| Post-restore settle | `UNAVAILABLE / SETTLING` for `DIAG_POST_RESTORE_SETTLE_FRAMES` frames, shown as the class word | None | MVP (dev) |

**Visual rules carried from Detailed Rules.** Text-first pages, not floating windows; fixed-width digits; numerals refresh at `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ` while markers and debug draws update per rendered frame (DO-28); the Overlay never displays a value in a colour that follows the lie (DO-27); the Overlay is immune to hallucination by construction (it sits above the Filter pass and is fed nothing by it), so there is no "the tool goes mad at stage 3" beat; the register is clinical and literal: no in-fiction theming and no player-mode vocabulary ("developer mode", "cheat", "assist") anywhere on screen (see Player Fantasy).

**Audio.** None. The Overlay owns no audio node, plays no cue on any severity, and does not sonify counters. Adaptive Audio & Tells registers a diagnostics channel whose voice counters, identity table and bus meters are **displayed here and never played** (adaptive-audio-and-tells.md:1511: "the one place audio values may be shown"). The tells that precede a breach (Pillar 2) belong to the world and to Adaptive Audio, not to this tool.

**Assets.** No sprite, shader, animation or sound asset. One font requirement only: a tabular (fixed-width digit) face at the HUD scale, owned by whichever GDD fixes the project's UI typography (HUD & Suit Layer, designed, its single `Theme` at HS-48; *provisional until the dev layer adopts it*). The dev strings are English-only, are not in any player string table, and are excluded from the release export with the rest of the dev root (DO-2).

📌 **Asset Spec** — Visual/Audio requirements are defined. After the art bible is approved, run `/asset-spec system:debug-overlay-true-values` to produce the per-asset visual specs; expect one tabular-digit font entry and no sprite, shader or audio assets.

## Game Feel

**Whose feel this is.** No player ever operates the Overlay, so there is no player feel to specify here: how a lie feels (the wrongness before the proof), how a breach feels (the tell, then chaos), how the HUD and instruments respond belong to the Perception Filter, the Pixel-Perfect Render Pipeline, the Attention System, HUD & Suit Layer (designed) and In-World Instruments (designed (this run)), and Adaptive Audio & Tells. This section specifies the **operator's feel**: the one sane seat aboard (the developer, QA or playtest observer), whose tool must be as immediate and as unremarkable as a light switch. The player-facing feel this tool protects is the promise that a gauge and the frost beside it disagree for a reason (Pillar 1); the Overlay is how that is proved, not part of what is felt.

### Feel Reference

**Minecraft's F3 screen, for the hotkey**: one key, text on a flat plate, no gameplay effect, readable at a glance, gone when released from the mind. **A compact net-graph-style strip, for the observer**: a single line that can be watched without being read. **Anti-reference:** a floaty, animated, skinned dev console that fades, slides and bounces (every transition is a frame in which the operator cannot tell what is true), and any "true sight" or assist-mode feel (the Overlay must feel like a diagnostic instrument, never like a power the player holds; Player Fantasy, "Rejected, by name" paragraph).

### Input Responsiveness

| Action | Max input-to-response latency | Frame budget (60 fps) | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `toggle` (`OFF` to `FULL`, or to `OFF`) | One rendered frame (DO-6: "takes effect within one rendered frame") | 1 frame | The page appears fully formed; no fade or slide. `T` does not change. |
| `page_next`, `page_prev` | One rendered frame | 1 frame | The page body changes; the numerals on it follow at the next refresh (at most 1 / `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ` s). |
| `mode` and the `reveal` chord | One rendered frame | 1 frame | `HIDDEN` to the strip is a deliberate two-key action and never accidental. |
| `mark` | One rendered frame | 1 frame | The stamp reads `T` at the key press; a refused 9th mark shows a `NOTE`. |
| `dump` | One rendered frame to the confirmation line; the file is written in the same frame for the text size involved | 1 frame to confirm | A dump is a pure function of the model; two dumps at one `T` while `PAUSED` are byte-identical. |
| A numeral on a page | At most 1 / `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ` = 100 ms behind the model | 6 frames at 10 Hz | The refresh cadence keeps digits readable; peak-hold (DO-18) guarantees a one-tick spike is shown at the next refresh and never lost. |
| A marker or debug-draw line | The same rendered frame | 1 frame | Markers and world debug draws follow the sim per frame, not per refresh. |

### Animation Feel Targets

Not applicable. The Overlay has no animation: no fade, slide, blink, pulse or easing on any element, because every animated frame is a frame the operator cannot trust as a reading, and an attention-grabbing flash on a `DEFECT` would break Pillar 2 for a tester in a shared room. State changes are instantaneous and text-only.

### Impact Moments

Not applicable. There is no hit-stop, screen shake, camera impact, controller rumble, time-scale change or sound, and none may be added (DO-30, the Player Fantasy guard rail "Never audible"). A `DEFECT` row's weight is carried by its tag text and its single red mark, not by any effect.

### Weight and Responsiveness Profile

- **Weight**: light and flat. It adds no visible frame-time cost (`OVERLAY_CPU_BUDGET_US` `[PLACEHOLDER]` 1000 us per rendered frame, a dev-template figure, F-7); the world must not stutter when the Overlay opens, because a stutter would be misread as a defect it is meant to find.
- **Player control**: total. Every action is a single deliberate key; there is no mode in which the Overlay moves on its own, steals focus (DO-5), or changes the sim (DO-1).
- **Snap quality**: crisp and binary for modes and pages; steady, never jittering, for numerals (fixed-width digits; the digit column does not move between refreshes).
- **Acceleration model**: instant on, instant off.
- **Failure texture**: when the tool cannot see something it says so by name (`UNAVAILABLE` with a reason, `n/a`, `insufficient samples (n/N)`, "dev template: indicative"); it never shows a clean `HONEST` or `OK` for an input it lacks. The operator should always be able to tell why a cell is empty.
- **Observer strip**: silent and peripheral; one line that can be watched from a seat away, with no sound and no change of state between refreshes.
- **Operator arc**: from "this report might be a defect" to "this row is `DELIBERATE`, the leak is present, the pipe is frosting" or to a named `DEFECT_*` with the field that proves it, inside one reproduction (Player Fantasy, fantasy failure condition).

### Feel Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] An operator opening the Overlay sees the page in the same frame as the key press and reports no perceptible delay (AC-DO-120).
- [ ] Numerals on `P-KERNEL` and `P-TRUTH` are readable without chasing: no digit jitter between two captures taken 100 ms apart (AC-DO-80).
- [ ] The world does not hitch when the Overlay is toggled on a machine at the reference spec, and no reviewer attributes a frame hitch to the tool (AC-DO-94, `[blocked: reference hardware]`).
- [ ] The observer strip can be read from a seat's distance and an observer logs Attention `phase` `TELL` without opening `FULL` (AC-DO-74).
- [ ] No reviewer uses the words "flashy", "distracting", "floaty" or "gamey" about the Overlay, and none asks for an animation or a sound on it.
- [ ] A reviewer shown a `DELIBERATE` row and a `DEFECT_NO_LEAK` row can tell which is the game working and which is a bug from the tag text alone, in a greyscale capture (AC-DO-81).

The cadence, cost and strip-placement figures cited in this section (`DIAG_REFRESH_HZ`, `OVERLAY_CPU_BUDGET_US`, `OVERLAY_STRIP_ANCHOR`) are Tuning Knobs rows, all `[PLACEHOLDER]`; this section restates none of them as a fact.

## UI Requirements

**No player-facing UI, and no UX flag.** The Overlay is a developer-only tool with no HUD, instrument, menu, screen or accessibility surface in any build a player can obtain (DO-2, DO-30; art bible R-7). Because it is not a player surface, `/ux-design` is not requested for it and **no "UX Flag" line is raised** *(orchestrator ruling R26: the Overlay's dev surfaces are specified as dev-tool requirements below, the register used by the Horror and Attention GDDs, rather than as a UX spec)*. The player-facing surfaces that sit beside it are owned elsewhere: the HUD and Suit Layer (layer 2), the Chart Table and menus (layer 3), the In-World Instruments, and the Accessibility and Settings screens; none of them hosts, instantiates, parents or links to an Overlay node, and none may show an Attention level, a sanity stage, a lie count or a verdict (AC-AT-66, AC-PF-51, AC-SN-146). If a playtest ever asks for the Overlay "as an option", the answer is the Player Fantasy's rejected framing and DO-30, not a UI task.

**Dev-tool surface requirements** (the layer-4 `Control` tree; the model is pure, DO-4):

| Information | Display location | Update frequency | Condition |
|---|---|---|---|
| Header: page name, viewer `PlayerId`, mode, kernel state, `T` (ticks and mm:ss), header severity (worst row on the page) | Top band of the Graphite plate, every page | Numerals at `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ`; kernel state on event | `FULL` |
| The seven pages `P-KERNEL`, `P-HOOKS`, `P-STATE`, `P-TRUTH`, `P-SANITY`, `P-ATTENTION`, `P-FRAME`, and `P-CHANNELS` | One page at a time, text rows (row, value, unit, severity tag), fixed-width digits | Numerals at `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ`; markers per rendered frame; `tick`-cadence figures peak-held | `FULL`; `page_next` / `page_prev` cycle and wrap |
| True value beside perceived value, class tag, `lie_id`, leak modes, `disc`, `tol_eff` | `P-TRUTH`, one block per selected ref, true in Paper White and perceived in Hull Grey | At `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ`; the active-lie marker per frame | A ref is listed (up to `DIAG_TRUTH_REFS_MAX`, "+n not shown") |
| Severity tag and (for `DEFECT`) the Alarm Red mark | Beside the row's value | Same as the row | The tag text always; the mark only for `DEFECT` and `NO_MASK` |
| The observer strip: `T`, kernel state, Attention `phase`, sanity `stage`, hash status | One line at `OVERLAY_STRIP_ANCHOR` (a corner) on a Graphite plate | At `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ` | `OBSERVER_STRIP` (and `reveal` from `HIDDEN`); contains no ref, ledger field or pair |
| The FAULT record `(T, type, issuer, host_order, block_id, assertion)` | Pinned on `P-KERNEL` and on the strip | On event | Kernel `FAULTED` in a dev build; not drawn in `HIDDEN` |
| `LOADING` banner; boot progress and failure row | Over the frozen last page; on `P-KERNEL` | On event | Kernel `LOADING` or `BOOTING` |
| Channel list and the selected channel's rows; status word per channel | `P-CHANNELS` | At `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ`; status on event | `FULL`; statuses `OK`, `PENDING`, `STALE`, `ERROR`, `SUSPENDED`, `REJECTED` |
| Row cursor (a marker beside the selected row, plus the pinned-ref flag) | The current page, `P-TRUTH` and `P-CHANNELS` | Per rendered frame | `FULL`; moved by `cursor_next` / `cursor_prev`, acted on by `activate` (DO-8) |
| World debug-draw toggles (one per declared draw) | `P-CHANNELS`, beside the owning channel; the lines draw on the layer-0 dev node | Toggle on event; lines per frame | A channel declared the draw; dev build only |
| Dump confirmation line; refused-mark `NOTE` | Header band, one line | On event | After `dump` or a refused `mark` |

**Requirements, each a dev-tool constraint (not a UX spec):**

1. **Scale and layer.** Root-viewport `CanvasLayer` at pipeline layer 4, scaled by `H_default = max(k, 3)` plus `OVERLAY_SCALE_OFFSET` (default 0), so the baseline at 1280x720 is `H` = 3; above the HUD (2) and the chart table and menus (3); no Filter pass, lighting, jolt or grade (DO-5, AC-DO-109).
2. **Palette and tags.** Only the six palette colours of AC-DO-79; severity is a text tag first; Alarm Red only as a mark beside `DEFECT` and `NO_MASK`; `WARN` uses no red; no violet, no flesh, ever (DO-27).
3. **Legibility.** Fixed-width digits on a Graphite plate of `OVERLAY_PLATE_ALPHA_PM` opacity `[PLACEHOLDER]`; contrast against the plate at least 4.5:1 for text and 3:1 for the red mark; a cap-height floor of 12 physical px at 1280x720 `[PLACEHOLDER, confirm with UX]` (AC-DO-80).
4. **Focus and mouse.** The Overlay never takes gameplay focus; non-interactive panels use `MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE`; a click through the Overlay area reaches the game (`[REF modules/ui.md]`, AC-DO-76). The only interactive controls are the debug-draw toggles on `P-CHANNELS`, and they are reachable only by the Adapter-forwarded dev actions (DO-8: `cursor_next`, `cursor_prev`, `activate`), never by a rebindable input.
5. **Input.** Ten dev-only actions, forwarded host-locally by the Controller's Input Adapter under the dev tag; never in the rebinding or accessibility enumerations (DO-8, AC-DO-18, AC-DO-70).
6. **Linear bars only.** Any bar is a linear bar with a needle mark; no ring, arc or dial (art bible 3.4, AC-DO-83).
7. **No audio, no motion.** No sound, no animation, no flash (Game Feel, the Player Fantasy guard rail "Never audible").
8. **Localization and accessibility.** Dev strings are English-only, outside every player string table, and absent from the release export; the Overlay is not offered as, and has no relationship to, any accessibility option (R-7); it appears in no screen-reader (AccessKit) tree of a release build because it is not in a release build, and in a dev build it adds no accessibility node (AC-DO-18).
9. **Evidence.** Retained screenshots of each screen touched (the seven pages and `P-CHANNELS`, the strip at two anchors, the `LOADING` banner, the `FAULTED` pin, a `REJECTED` and a `SUSPENDED` channel, and `HIDDEN`) in `production/qa/evidence/debug-overlay/` (AC-DO-80 to 82, 89, 152).

## Acceptance Criteria

Every criterion below is a GIVEN / WHEN / THEN a QA tester or a headless runner can pass or fail without opening this GDD's other sections: the terms it uses are defined in the **Test glossary**, the knob values it relies on are in the **Knob defaults** table, and the inputs of every classifier row are written out in full. Rule ids (`DO-n`), formula ids (`F-n`) and edge-case ids (`E-DO-nn`) are secondary tags in square brackets; sibling rules are named by their own id (Kernel CR-9, Filter PF-41, Sanity SN-28) and a `file:line` cite is a second tag only, because line numbers move on every sibling edit. The Overlay is an infrastructure system with no gameplay math of its own, so most criteria are Logic (automated, BLOCKING) and the player-visible ones are UI/Visual (a retained screenshot, BLOCKING at every `qa.level`).

**Tags.** `(auto)` an automated headless test in `tests/unit/debug_overlay/` or `tests/integration/debug_overlay/`. `(lint)` a source, asset or file-list scan run in CI. `(review)` a checklist with a **named reviewer**, retained in `production/qa/evidence/debug-overlay/`. `(manual)` retained evidence (screenshot, recording or log) in `production/qa/evidence/debug-overlay/`. `(spike)` a time-boxed engine-behaviour proof for an `[UNV, HIGH]` assumption (no engine-reference file exists for export, debugging or scripting). `[blocked: X]` marks a criterion that cannot pass until `X` exists; each has a stub-based interim test so the gate does not fall out silently (see Gaps). **Gate level:** `rigor: full` and `qa.level` unset, so Logic and Integration evidence is BLOCKING; UI and Visual evidence is BLOCKING at every level ("tests are waived, the look is not", coding standards); Config/Data rows are ADVISORY. **Spike rule:** each spike has a time box of one working day `[PLACEHOLDER]` and passes when its report file exists in `production/qa/spikes/` and states either "confirmed as designed" or a named ADR or GDD amendment request; a spike never silently changes a design rule.

**Determinism and harness (applies to every time-dependent row).** The test drives the Overlay's process callback with an **injected frame delta** (default 1/60 s) and steps the real kernel with its own tick driver; no criterion reads a wall clock, and the Overlay under test reads none (AC-DO-153). "N frames" means N calls of the process callback; "a refresh" means the callback on which the accumulated delta crosses `1 / DIAG_REFRESH_HZ`. Seams requested of the lead-programmer (not decided here; each has the fallback in Gaps): **T-1** an injectable dev-tag provider in the composition root; **T-2** the kernel exposing the calling subscriber id at mutator and `WriteScope` entry; **T-3** the injectable frame delta and fake clock above; **T-4** stubbable Filter (`perceived`, counters, `t_return_max`, `q_display`), Sanity ledger and Graph/Suit `true_value` sources behind the owners' published read views; **T-5** a harness hook that forces `state_hash` every tick independent of the Overlay (Kernel G4); **T-6** call-spy seams on the Filter's `perceived` and on the Overlay's handler and classifier.

**Test glossary** (one line per term; the definition is the one in Formulas F-1).

| Term | Meaning in these criteria |
|---|---|
| `tv`, `perceived`, `displayed_value` | `tv` is the owner's `true_value`; `perceived` is the Filter's `Perceived` record for `(ref, viewer)`; `displayed_value` is its value, equal to `tv` exactly on the honest path |
| `disc` | `abs(round_half_up(displayed_value) - tv)` in the ref's own unit (`bp`, `mC`, `dkPa`, `permille`); 0 or 1 for binary-frame refs |
| `tol(kind)`, `q_display`, `tol_eff` | `tol` is the owner's dirty-key quantum for the field (Graph `hp` 250 bp, `T` 1000 mC, `P` 10 dkPa, `p_o2` 5 dkPa, store 20 permille, intensity 100 permille; Suit reserve and cell 5 percent of max, `integrity_bp` 250 bp; 0 for `o2_pm`, `n_o2`, `n_inert`); `q_display` is one per mille of the instrument range (Filter PF-45 calibration, published by the Filter, registry `filter_counters`); `tol_eff = max(tol, q_display)`; `disc == tol_eff` is **inside** |
| `ledger_active` | the viewer's `sanity.ledger` holds a row for the ref's subject in state `ACTIVE`; Sanity `PENDING` and the ended states are not active |
| `lie_age`, `reach_age` | `lie_age = run_now - active_run` in Sanity runs (5 per second); `reach_age = ceil(600 x (end_run - active_run) / 1000)` runs (`GLIDE_REACH_FRAC` 0.6); binary-frame refs use `SWAP_LEAD_S` x 5 runs |
| `n_eq`, `n_skew`, `t_ret` | consecutive rendered frames with `disc <= tol_eff`, counted only from the first frame on which `lie_age >= reach_age` (0 before it; binary-frame refs: from `SWAP_LEAD_S` x 5 runs); consecutive rendered frames with `ledger_active` XOR (`lie_id != 0` and `motion_mode != RETURN`); seconds since `RETURN` began, counted in kernel ticks |
| `t_return_max(lie)` | the longest honest return, published by the Filter per lie (depends on `rm`); the Overlay never restates it |
| Classes | `UNAVAILABLE`, `SKEW`, `DEFECT_NO_LEAK`, `DEFECT_SILENT_LIE`, `DEFECT_BYPASS`, `DELIBERATE` (tagged `LIE` or `ONSET`), `RETURNING`, `HONEST`, `DEFECT_UNCAUSED`; severity words are exactly `OK`, `NOTE`, `WARN`, `DEFECT` |
| `P-KERNEL`, `P-HOOKS`, `P-STATE`, `P-TRUTH`, `P-SANITY`, `P-ATTENTION`, `P-FRAME`, `P-CHANNELS` | the seven MVP pages and the generic channel page (DO-15) |
| Modes | `OFF`, `FULL`, `OBSERVER_STRIP`, `HIDDEN` (DO-6) |

**Knob defaults used by these criteria** (all `[PLACEHOLDER]`, from Tuning Knobs; a criterion that names a knob uses this value).

| Knob | Value | Knob | Value |
|---|---|---|---|
| `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ` | 10 | `DIAG_CHANNEL_STALE_S` | 5 s |
| `DIAG_SILENT_LIE_GRACE_FRAMES` | 120 | `DIAG_PANEL_ERR_LATCH_N` | 3 |
| `DIAG_SKEW_GRACE_FRAMES` | 24 | `DIAG_TRUTH_REFS_MAX` | 16 |
| `DIAG_POST_RESTORE_SETTLE_FRAMES` | 6 | `DIAG_CMD_LOG_ROWS` | 12 |
| `DIAG_HOOK_WINDOW_RUNS` / `DIAG_P99_MIN_SAMPLES` | 600 / 100 | `DIAG_DUMP_MAX_FILES` / `DIAG_MARK_MAX` | 20 / 8 |
| `DIAG_DIRTY_WARN_PM` / `DIAG_DIRTY_ALERT_PM` | 500 / 750 | `DIAG_FRAME_WARN_FPS` | 20 |
| `DIAG_QUEUE_WARN_PM` / `DIAG_LAG_WARN_TICKS` | 500 / 4 | `DIAG_FILTER_CPU_WINDOW_FRAMES` | 300 |
| `OVERLAY_CPU_BUDGET_US` | 1000 | `DIAG_SELF_COST_WINDOW_FRAMES` | 300 |

**Fixtures.** `FX-REPLAY-A`: a scripted, seeded run of 1800 ticks (30 s) on the MVP slice (home-ship 5 compartments, 8 faults, one `FALSE_OK` lie active between tick 600 and tick 1200), run Overlay-absent, `HIDDEN` and `FULL`, with every page cycled every 60 frames. `FX-CLASS`: the classifier fixture set of Formulas F-1 (Graph `hp`, `tol` 250 bp, `q_display` 10 bp, `tol_eff` 250; `P` in dkPa; `T` in mC), one data table, one row per class, the runner reporting the failing row id (the AC-PF-40 pattern); its expected classes are the F-1 example rows. The `hp` dial is a synthetic fixture: Instruments holds `strain` (hp) out of the MVP manifest (in-world-instruments-and-inspect-view.md:227), so the MVP live refs are `p_o2`, `P`, `T`, `stock`, `load`. `FX-RETURN`: Graph `hp` 0 to 10000 bp, needle `arc_deg` 240 (Instruments IW rule at in-world-instruments-and-inspect-view.md:201) so 1 deg = 41.67 bp, `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE` 3.0 deg/s = 125 bp/s (75 bp/s at `rm` 0.6), `disc_end` 2500 bp at removal, `tv` 2400 held unless a row says otherwise, refresh at 10 Hz.

### 3. The classifier `desync_classifier` (DO-20 to DO-23; formula F-1)

Test file `tests/unit/debug_overlay/test_desync_classifier.gd`; the runner reports the failing row id. Every row carries its full inputs (no row depends on a neighbour). The expected class of each row comes from the F-1 class table written independently of the code under test.

- **AC-DO-22 (auto)**: GIVEN `P` with `tv` 1013 dkPa, displayed 1013, `lie_id` 0, not `ledger_active` WHEN the classifier runs THEN the class is `HONEST`, severity `OK`, no mark. `[F-1 example 1]`

- **AC-DO-25 (auto)**: GIVEN `hp` with `tv` 2400, displayed 2650 (`disc` 250, inside `tol_eff` 250), `ledger_active`, `lie_id` 7, `leak_modes` 3, a 200-run lie (`reach_age` 120), `lie_age` 40 runs, `n_eq` 0 (it does not count before the gate) WHEN the classifier runs THEN the class is `DELIBERATE` tagged `ONSET`, never a defect. `[F-1 example 3]`

GIVEN a 201-run lie (`reach_age` = ceil(120.6) = 121) WHEN (`lie_age`, `n_eq`) is (120, 300) THEN `DELIBERATE` tagged `ONSET`, and (132, 121) THEN `DEFECT_SILENT_LIE` (proves the ceiling). `[F-1 example 6; CP-5]`

Contrast row: the same lie with `disc` pinned at 0 (never rendered) is `DELIBERATE` tagged `ONSET` until `lie_age` 120, then `DEFECT_SILENT_LIE` once `n_eq` exceeds 120, about 26 s. For a binary-frame ref the age gate is `SWAP_LEAD_S` x 5 runs, read from the Filter's value; a `FALSE_OK` or `FALSE_FAIL` on a needle uses the reach age above, as this row does. `[F-1 silent-lie worked example; CP-5]`

GIVEN `disc` = `tol_eff` WHEN sampled THEN `n_eq` keeps running and the step test passes; GIVEN `disc` = `tol_eff` + 1 THEN `n_eq` resets to 0 and the step test fails. ### 5. The channel registration contract (DO-12 to DO-14)

- **AC-DO-65 (auto)**: GIVEN a declaration with `channel_id` `fx_channel`, owner `Fixture`, and three rows (name, unit, kind in {`COUNTER`, `GAUGE`, `FLAG`, `TEXT`, `TABLE`}, severity rule) WHEN registered in `BOOTING` THEN `P-CHANNELS` lists it and renders each row with the declared unit. Table-driven negatives, one row per invalid declaration, each yields status `REJECTED` with an error row naming the channel and the fault: a missing `channel_id`; a duplicate `channel_id`; an unknown row kind (`GRAPH`); a unit embedded in a row name (`fuel_ms`) when the owner's registry name does not carry it; a name breaking the snake_case / owner-prefix convention (`FuelMs!`). The Overlay does not crash and the other channels render. `[DO-12, DO-13]`
- **AC-DO-66 (auto)**: GIVEN a channel registered in `BOOTING` WHEN the Overlay is opened at any later time THEN the channel appears with its rows; GIVEN a registration attempted after `BOOTING` (the BOOTING-only window is this GDD's own rule, adopted by analogy with Kernel CR-6, which covers blocks, command types and hooks only) THEN it is refused and never appears, and the expected-channel list (the static 15-channel list of AC-DO-69) shows `NOTE absent` for it; GIVEN a channel first drawn before its registration has completed THEN it shows `PENDING`. `[E-DO-38; DO-14]`
- **AC-DO-67 (auto)**: GIVEN a severity rule that names an unknown severity word (`ERROR`) WHEN registered THEN the channel is `REJECTED`; only `OK`, `NOTE`, `WARN` and `DEFECT` exist; GIVEN a `DEFECT` rule that cites no owner rule THEN `REJECTED`; a `DEFECT` rule that cites an owner rule is accepted and evaluated. `[DO-13]`
- **AC-DO-68 (auto)**: GIVEN a channel whose `pull()` throws `DIAG_PANEL_ERR_LATCH_N` 3 times in a row WHEN the model refreshes THEN one `DEFECT` row names the `channel_id` and the error class, the other channels render, the channel is `SUSPENDED` until the next mode change, and the kernel stays `RUNNING`. `[DO-14; Kernel subscriber isolation]`
- **AC-DO-69 (auto)**: GIVEN one registered channel per sibling listed in Dependencies B (Horror, Body, Lighting, Emitter, Layout, Save/Load, Interaction, Controller, Web Map, Audio, Assembly, Boarding, Suit beyond `P-TRUTH`, plus In-World Instruments and HUD & Suit Layer; 15 expected) with stub rows WHEN `P-CHANNELS` is built THEN the channels list, selecting each shows its rows, a debug-draw toggle exists only for channels that declared one, and registering more than `DIAG_CHANNELS_MAX` 64 channels or `DIAG_ROWS_PER_CHANNEL_MAX` 64 rows yields `REJECTED` rows, not a crash. `[DO-12, DO-26]`
- **AC-DO-143 (auto)**: the channel status machine, with the injected clock: GIVEN a channel with no update for 4.9 s THEN `OK`; for 5.0 s (`DIAG_CHANNEL_STALE_S`) THEN `STALE` / `NOTE` with the last values marked; WHEN the next `pull()` succeeds THEN `OK`. GIVEN `pull()` throws twice and then succeeds THEN the channel is `OK` and not suspended (the failure count resets); GIVEN it throws three times in a row THEN `SUSPENDED` with a `DEFECT` row, it stays suspended through further refreshes, and the next mode change resumes pulling. `[DO-14; channel status machine]`

### 9. Performance (DO-29; formula F-7)

Wall-clock rows are `[blocked: reference hardware]` until the technical director names the two reference machines (OQ-PF-25) and are indicative on the developer machine until then. The Overlay's numbers are dev-template numbers and gate nothing in Release.

- **AC-DO-94 (manual)** `[blocked: reference hardware]`: `overlay_cpu_us` <= `OVERLAY_CPU_BUDGET_US` 1000 `[PLACEHOLDER]`: GIVEN stage 3 with four lies, all seven pages cycling, 15 channels registered and 256-key batches on 3 blocks WHEN the Overlay's CPU per rendered frame is measured over 3600 frames with the engine profiler (until the kernel's diagnostics timer is granted, DO-33c) THEN the **mean over each `DIAG_SELF_COST_WINDOW_FRAMES` 300-frame window** is <= 1000 us (the F-7 gate); the P99 and the maximum are logged for information and gate nothing.
- **AC-DO-94b (auto)**: GIVEN stubbed timer terms `t_copy_us` 30, `t_model_us` 400, `t_view_us` 150 at 60 fps and `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ` 10 WHEN F-7 is evaluated THEN `overlay_cpu_us` = (30 x 60 + 400 x 10 + 150 x 60) / 60 = 246.7 us (`OK`); the same terms at 20 fps give (30 x 60 + 400 x 10 + 150 x 20) / 20 = 440 us; the criterion proves the arithmetic only. `[F-7]`
- **AC-DO-95 (auto, indicative, no gate)**: GIVEN the Overlay `HIDDEN` WHEN the AC-DO-94 run is repeated THEN the Overlay CPU per frame is logged against the `FULL` figure; the 10 percent ratio is a `[PLACEHOLDER]` target proposed in OQ-DO-14 and is not a pass/fail threshold until the technical director adopts it.
- **AC-DO-96 (auto, indicative, no gate)**: GIVEN a P5 batch of 256 keys on 3 blocks WHEN the handler runs THEN the work is a bounded copy plus a dirty mark and the memory growth over 3600 ticks is logged; the 0.05 ms handler time and 64 KiB growth figures are `[PLACEHOLDER]` targets proposed in OQ-DO-14, not gates until adopted. `[DO-3]`
- **AC-DO-97 (auto)**: GIVEN a throttled run in which the mean `overlay_cpu_us` exceeds the budget over one `DIAG_SELF_COST_WINDOW_FRAMES` window WHEN observed THEN the self-cost row is `WARN`, the refresh rate is strictly lower than before, and the collected tick count equals the kernel's tick count (collection is never skipped). `[E-DO-35]`
- **AC-DO-98 (auto)**: GIVEN a release-mode Overlay-absent run WHEN memory and node count are sampled THEN the Overlay contributes zero (nothing loaded); "zero cost in Release" is the file-list fact of AC-DO-13, not a runtime branch. `[DO-2]`
- **AC-DO-148 (auto)**: GIVEN a configured `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ` of 10 and a run whose window mean is over budget in three consecutive windows WHEN observed THEN the refresh rate steps 10, then 5, then 2 Hz and never below 2 Hz; GIVEN two consecutive under-budget windows THEN the rate returns one rung up the same ladder (2 to 5), and two further consecutive under-budget windows return it 5 to 10 (capped at `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ`); a single over-budget spike inside an otherwise under-budget window does not change the rate; collection is unchanged throughout. `[E-DO-35; F-7; recovery rule is a placeholder]`

### 11. Cross-system criteria (dependency contracts end to end; real owner modules except where the neighbour has no file)

- **AC-DO-107 (auto)**: Kernel (hard): GIVEN the real kernel and 600 ticks WHEN 5 commands are queued, a command is rejected and an injected 5 ms hook runs THEN queue depth, the reject counter and `step_over_budget` move as expected and the Overlay model equals the kernel's values for every item in the kern:915 list; the count of `step_over_budget` ticks in `FX-REPLAY-A` with the Overlay present equals the Overlay-absent count on the developer machine (indicative; the hard wall-clock gate stays `[blocked: reference hardware]`).
- **AC-DO-108 (auto)**: Perception Filter (hard): GIVEN the real Filter, one Graph ref, one Suit ref and a Sanity-issued lie WHEN read THEN `true_value` and `perceived` for the same ref appear with the six PF-41 fields and the 14 counters, `state_hash` excludes the perceived channel, and `remote_queries` stays 0 on a solo run.
- **AC-DO-109 (auto)**: Pipeline (soft): GIVEN the running scene tree WHEN inspected THEN the Overlay `CanvasLayer` is layer 4 in the root viewport above the HUD (2) and the chart table and menus (3), the Filter pass is below it, it scales at `H_default = max(k, 3)` plus `OVERLAY_SCALE_OFFSET`, and under camera jolt, a dark compartment and stage 3 the Overlay-region pixel diff with the Filter on versus off is 0. `[DO-5]`
- **AC-DO-110 (auto)**: Life-Support Graph: GIVEN the real Graph with fault F1 injected WHEN read THEN `true_value(kind, id)` equals the `graph.state` value for compartment `T`, `P`, `p_o2` and store, `block_hash()` is shown, only the hook ids `graph.propagate.power`, `.atmo`, `.failure` appear, and `graph_saturations[field]` all zeros reads `OK`; GIVEN a forced `DT_CLAMP` hit (a thermal step above `MAX_DT` 2000 mC) THEN that field increments and the row reads `WARN`. `[P-STATE table]`
- **AC-DO-111 (auto)**: Suit: GIVEN the five keys (`suit.o2`, `suit.reserve`, `suit.integrity`, `suit.warmth`, `suit.charge`) for PlayerId 1 WHEN read at MVP THEN all five are shown and classify `HONEST`.
- **AC-DO-112 (auto)**: Sanity: GIVEN `sanity_view(player_id)`, `sanity.state` and `sanity.ledger` read (SN-28) and a stage set by the **owner's** debug API from a test harness WHEN the next refresh runs THEN the stage is displayed within one refresh, the Overlay exposes no `force_*` control, and the `FORCED` row reads the `sanity.state` `forced` flag directly: a forced seat shows the `FORCED` tag and reads `NOTE`, and level alone never sets the tag. `[OQ-DO-4 (resolved), OQ-DO-11]`
- **AC-DO-113 (auto)**: Attention: GIVEN `attention.state` and `attention.view` WHEN read THEN the level and `tell_remaining` appear only in the Overlay root (AC-DO-17), the TellAudit result is displayed not computed, and the raw level never reaches a `PRESENTATION` subscriber (subscriber-class check).
- **AC-DO-114 (auto)**: Save/Load: GIVEN a save-restore cycle with the Overlay open WHEN it completes THEN the SL-12 reproduction compare result, the `save_status` transitions and the forced hash at restore are shown (AC-DO-91).
- **AC-DO-115 (auto)** `[blocked: OQ-DO-6]`: Controller: GIVEN the dev action set forwarded host-locally (AC-DO-71) WHEN the `Input` lint runs (AC-DO-70) THEN it passes.
- **AC-DO-116 (auto)**: Horror, Emitter, Lighting, Layout, Body, Assembly, Web Map, Interaction, Audio, Boarding, HUD: GIVEN each owner's registered channel and any declared draw toggle WHEN rendered THEN it appears only through the Overlay root's dev node, read-only (`blocked(actor_id)` diagnostic only), with no flesh or violet tint, and Audio's counters are displayed, never sonified.
- **AC-DO-117 (review)**: No-contract trio: GIVEN Failure & Trail, Item & Module Database and Derelict Authoring WHEN the **producer** reviews the Dependencies and Cross-References sections THEN none has a Dependencies row and each is listed under "no contract" in Cross-References; the checklist is retained.

### Gaps / untestable as written

1. **Wall-clock rows `[blocked: reference hardware]`.** AC-DO-94 (and the indicative AC-DO-95 and AC-DO-96, and the wall-clock half of AC-DO-107) cannot become gates until the technical director names the two reference machines (OQ-PF-25); until then they are indicative on the developer machine, and the Overlay's figure is a dev-template number that gates nothing in Release (DO-19).
2. **Blocked on a neighbour amendment, each with an interim test.** AC-DO-71 and AC-DO-115 (Controller adds the ten dev actions, OQ-DO-6; interim: a stub Adapter and the host-local entry point). AC-DO-5 (kernel exposes the calling subscriber id, seam T-2; interim: the writes-disabled enforcement for `DIAGNOSTIC`). AC-DO-134 (the Filter's ref-to-subject map, PF-9, is provisional; interim: a stub map). AC-DO-18 screen halves (Accessibility Settings and Settings & Input Rebinding have no file; interim: the action-map and node-tree enumerators).
3. **Subjective or process-bound evidence.** The tester-screen oracle risk is a process risk the code cannot remove: AC-DO-74 proves only that the scan of one 10-minute capture finds no Overlay pixel, and AC-DO-75 proves only that the protocol says so; a stream that shows `FULL` voids that session and no automated criterion can prevent it. AC-DO-80's "legible at arm's length" is an observer sign-off, backed by the measurable contrast, cap-height and jitter checks; the cap-height figure is a `[PLACEHOLDER]` to confirm with UX. AC-DO-81 depends on the art director's ratification of red-as-mark (OQ-DO-9).
4. **Design-time assumptions the criteria cannot confirm.** `DEFECT_BYPASS` has no real probe until IW-38's probe exists in a build (P-2): AC-DO-40 uses a stub, and the production class stays `ABSENT`. The `SCANNER` ref has no true source and is `UNAVAILABLE / NO_TRUE_SOURCE` (OQ-DO-2). The throttle recovery rule of AC-DO-148 (two under-budget windows) and the 10 percent, 0.05 ms and 64 KiB figures of AC-DO-95 and AC-DO-96 are placeholders proposed in OQ-DO-14, not adopted values.
5. **Spikes `(spike)` are engine-behaviour proofs, not behaviour criteria.** AC-DO-11, 12, 78, 86 and 87 answer `[UNV, HIGH]` questions (export filter, feature tag, second window, debug-draw colour under stage 3, render-info fields); each is time-boxed to one working day `[PLACEHOLDER]` and ends in a report or an ADR/GDD amendment request. The engine reference has no scripting, core, export or debugging file.
6. **Testability seams T-1 to T-6** (the preamble) are requests to the lead-programmer. Without T-1, T-3 and T-4 the integration group degrades to manual playtest evidence, which weakens the BLOCKING gate; without T-5 the inertness comparison depends on the kernel's own hash cadence.

## Open Questions

**User-decision items (first).** Autonomous mode answered every framing, approach and section-approval question by taking the recommended option and logging it; none is a blocking user question, but each of these is a decision the user may reverse, and none changes a pillar: (1) **Four modes** with `HIDDEN` as the playtest-session default and a two-step reveal (DO-6 to DO-8), accepting that a stream showing `FULL` or the strip voids a session; (2) **Alarm Red as a mark only**, never a fill (DO-27, ratification by the art director is OQ-DO-9); (3) **the Overlay is a display tool and writes nothing**, so `force_*` stays in the owners' debug APIs behind the dev console (DO-1); (4) **the kernel computes the hash, the Overlay displays it**, with a proposed kernel cadence knob (DO-17, DO-33); (5) **the classifier's closure rule**: a return that reaches `disc` 0 is `HONEST` on that frame even if `motion_mode` is still `RETURN` (DO-21, final authoring); (6) the rulings on the binary-frame age gate, `UNAVAILABLE` precedence, the static expected-channel list, mark overflow and the refresh-tick granularity of the stage stopwatch (DO-21, DO-31, E-DO-38, F-9); (7) the throttle sequence 10, 5, 2 Hz with a two-window recovery (E-DO-35, F-7).

Every other request in this section is made of an owner and is **not** a decision of this GDD: nothing here edits another system's file, the registry, the index or the concept document, and nothing in the Overlay's design depends on a request being granted (each has a stated fallback). Ids `OQ-DO-n` are cited from the rules, formulas, edge cases and acceptance criteria. **Column mapping:** *Owner* is the system or role that must answer (the table's "user / agent / GDD" owner kind: GDD means a neighbouring system's GDD, agent means a named specialist, user means the project owner); *Where it bites* names the rules and criteria affected and the target stage (next GDD, ADR, playtest or architecture) in its last words.
